      "solution": "**Identify.** Which of the following is against the rules of ICBN?\n\n**Concept.** NCERT “Biology General” fact for this stem: **Generic and specific names should be written starting with small letters.**.\n\n**Work.** Link the stem to the verified wording “Generic and specific names should be written starting with small letters.”, then reject each distractor that violates that fact or the matching formula.\n\n**Option scan.**\n✗ (1) Hand written scientific names should be underlined.\n✗ (2) Every species should have a generic name and a specific epithet.\n✗ (3) Scientific names are in Latin and should be italized.\n✓ (4) Generic and specific names should be written starting with small letters.\n\n**Hence proved.** The verified option wording is **(4) Generic and specific names should be written starting with small letters.**.\n\n**Eliminate.**\n✗ (1) Hand written scientific names should be underlined. — Conflicts with “Generic and specific names should be written starting with small lett…”.\n✗ (2) Every species should have a generic name and a specific epithet. — Conflicts with “Generic and specific names should be written starting with small lett…”.\n✗ (3) Scientific names are in Latin and should be italized. — Conflicts with “Generic and specific names should be written starting with small lett…”.",

      "solution": "**Identify.** In the process of transcription in Eukaryotes, the RNA polymerase I transcribes -\n\n**Concept.** NCERT “Genetics & Biotech” fact for this stem: **rRNAs - 28 S, 18 S and 5.8 S**.\n\n**Work.** Link the stem to the verified wording “rRNAs - 28 S, 18 S and 5.8 S”, then reject each distractor that violates that fact or the matching formula.\n\n**Option scan.**\n✗ (1) mRNA with additional processing, capping and tailing\n✗ (2) tRNA, 5 S rRNA and snRNAs\n✓ (3) rRNAs - 28 S, 18 S and 5.8 S\n✗ (4) Precursor of mRNA, hnRNA\n\n**Hence proved.** The verified option wording is **(3) rRNAs - 28 S, 18 S and 5.8 S**.\n\n**Eliminate.**\n✗ (1) mRNA with additional processing, capping and tailing — Conflicts with “rRNAs - 28 S, 18 S and 5.8 S”.\n✗ (2) tRNA, 5 S rRNA and snRNAs — Conflicts with “rRNAs - 28 S, 18 S and 5.8 S”.\n✗ (4) Precursor of mRNA, hnRNA — Conflicts with “rRNAs - 28 S, 18 S and 5.8 S”.",
